None if no definitions\\n  are missing.\\n- **Domain files and directories:** The structure reported by\\n  `scaffold-domain`, created only if the selected domain is missing.\\n- **Transient working material:** The inventory updated with generated IDs\\n  and file paths. Retain it under the same conditions as [Describe the intended outcome](DEFINE_CHANGE.md#describe-the-intended-outcome).\\n\\n**Actions:**\\n\\n1. Read `Design simplicity` in `docs/engineering/ARTIFACT_AUTHORING.md`.\\n2. Select the domain for each missing definition from the inventory. For a\\n   missing domain, preview its creation with the domain commands below.\\n3. Inspect the proposed domain paths. Resolve any reported conflict before\\n   running the domain creation command.\\n4. Select the first missing artifact type from the table below. Skip types\\n   already covered by unchanged existing artifacts.\\n5. Run the artifact preview command. Inspect its type, destination path, and\\n   `allocated_id`.\\n6. Run the artifact creation command with that exact ID. If a conflict occurs,\\n   repeat the preview; do not overwrite an existing record.\\n7. Open the returned file. Complete its title, owners, dates, content, and\\n   declared links. Replace all template placeholders.\\n8. Apply `### Checklist` under the matching `## TYPE` heading in\\n   `docs/engineering/ARTIFACT_AUTHORING.md`.\\n9. Read the completed draft. Record its ID and path in the inventory.\\n10. Repeat actions 4\\u20139 for each remaining missing definition.\\n\\n| Order | Artifact | TYPE | Content | Declared links |\\n| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |\\n| 1 | Intent (INT) | `intent` | Problem, intended outcome, affected users, and scope limits. | None required here. |\\n| 2 | Capability (CAP) | `capability` | The ability needed to achieve the outcome. | `derives_from` \\u2192 intent (INT). |\\n| 3 | Requirement (REQ) | `requirement` | Observable behavior, conditions, and acceptance criteria. | `derives_from` \\u2192 capability (CAP). |\\n| 4 | Specification (SPEC) | `specification` | Detailed behavior, constraints, failure behavior, and checkable examples. | `specifies` \\u2192 requirement (REQ). |\\n| 5 | Architecture (ARCH), when applicable | `architecture` | Components, boundaries, and the architecture decision assessment. | `addresses` \\u2192 requirement (REQ); `conforms_to` \\u2192 specification (SPEC). |\\n| 6 | Architecture decision (ADR), when required | `adr` | Context, alternatives, chosen design, and consequences. | `decides` \\u2192 architecture (ARCH). |\\n\\nRecord an unresolved design choice for [Record unresolved decisions (DEC)](RISKS_AND_DECISIONS.md#record-unresolved-decisions-dec). Do not present a recommendation\\nas an agreed architecture decision (ADR). Prepare verification contracts (VER)\\nin [Define how the result will be verified](DRAFT_DEFINITIONS.md#define-how-the-result-will-be-verified).\\n\\n**Harness commands:**\\n\\nFor a missing domain, use the first command in action 2 and the second only\\nafter reviewing the preview in action 3:\\n\\n```text\\nharnessctl scaffold-domain REPO --domain DOMAIN --dry-run --json\\nharnessctl scaffold-domain REPO --domain DOMAIN --json\\n```\\n\\nFor each missing artifact, use this **creation sequence** in actions 5\\u20136.\\nReplace `TYPE` with the table value. Replace `ARTIFACT-ID` in the second\\ncommand with the first command's `allocated_id`:\\n\\n```text\\nharnessctl create-artifact REPO --domain DOMAIN --type TYPE --dry-run --json\\nharnessctl create-artifact REPO --domain DOMAIN --type TYPE --id ARTIFACT-ID --json\\n```\\n\\nThe preview writes nothing and does not reserve an ID. Creation writes one\\nincomplete template and returns its path. Content completion is a separate\\nediting action. The JSON output does not contain the authoring checklist.\\n\\n**Completion:** Each missing definition has a completed draft with an ID and\\npath. Unresolved choices are identified. If no definitions are missing,\\nrecord `None needed` in the transient inventory.\\n\\n**Later use:** These definitions supply the verification contracts (VER) in\\n[Define how the result will be verified](DRAFT_DEFINITIONS.md#define-how-the-result-will-be-verified) and the governing links in work orders (WO) in [Prepare work orders (WO) linking the definitions, scope, planned work, and verification requirements](DRAFT_WORK_ORDERS.md#prepare-work-orders-wo-linking-the-definitions-scope-planned-work-and-verification-requirements). Their acceptance\\nis handled in the authorization process.\\n\\n\", \"content_sha256\": \"c38865671a81429dd291d9f9bb333deae02a4098d4afcf034f293ee919e990e0\"}]}, {\"resource\": \"docs/engineering/ARTIFACT_AUTHORING.md\", \"path\": \"C:\\\\Users\\\\mathi\\\\Documents\\\\Codex\\\\2026-09-20\\\\verity-plane-plugin-verity-plane-se\\\\work\\\\hag-hosted-next28-20261011\\\\claude-232\\\\native\\\\inputs\\\\released-resources\\\\docs\\\\engineering\\\\ARTIFACT_AUTHORING.md\", \"release\": {\"archive_sha256\": \"cb35c3c4eb51fe0fca575a9340b1cba6cd63b5f7133a0ebeb3fd32b4ed863053\", \"payload_sha256\": \"0e7b7c2bc10b06150c6c1ebe88d796d047f5d906bdfb5ef4e00914a56c79e7ff\", \"version\": \"0.22.1\"}, \"sha256\": \"71de2cc92a470e2da25d525e3ad002b77faedb6ef0e69e2fa476a2fa4cb78ff5\", \"bytes\": 11691, \"sections\": [{\"heading\": \"work_order\", \"title\": \"work_order\", \"anchor\": \"work_order\", \"start_line\": 136, \"end_line\": 146, \"content\": \"## work_order\\n\\n### Checklist\\n\\n- `[execution_scope].paths` is exact: files or component-prefix directories, nothing wider.\\n- `[assurance]` is classified and its rationale names what later decisions depend on.\\n- In scope and out of scope are both stated; the decision envelope says what the implementer may and may not decide.\\n- Stop conditions and the completion report format are stated.\\n- Is every planned change needed for the agreed outcome? Include removal or adjustment of obsolete behavior, tests and instructions within the approved scope.\\n- Inspect supporting paths before approval and assess the supplied plan through [Draft work orders](harness/DRAFT_WORK_ORDERS.md#prepare-work-orders-wo-linking-the-definitions-scope-planned-work-and-verification-requirements). Keep reasons in Expected change surface; do not add a separate planning artifact.\\n\\n\", \"content_sha256\": \"e8d3be9bfae6d0679a08544ac587fa73e1a26e026d23373c29e1927e0803e7b8\"}, {\"heading\": \"design-simplicity\", \"title\": \"Design simplicity\", \"anchor\": \"design-simplicity\", \"start_line\": 21, \"end_line\": 58, \"content\": \"## Design simplicity\\n\\nFor unfinished work, `harnessctl validate-draft REPO --artifact ID --json`\\nreports whether one supported draft has an admissible shape. Only exact values\\nat the same type and field in this evaluator's canonical template, and missing\\nor empty required authoring links, can be reported as incomplete. Invalid\\nidentity, dates, metadata shapes, actual endpoints, lifecycle history and\\ndisposition are errors. A missing non-template ID is an error. Background\\nfindings remain visible; this result makes no whole-repository validity claim.\\nComplete the authoring checklist and pass ordinary approval gates before\\nrequesting approval. Draft admission grants no authority to write or approve.\\n\\nChoose the least complex design that satisfies the agreed requirements and\\nconstraints. Justify material added complexity by the need it serves and why\\na simpler approach is insufficient. Consider the effort to understand, build,\\ntest, operate and change the whole solution. Defer speculative needs. Review\\nboth the necessity of the specified behavior and the simplicity of its\\nimplementation. Preserve correctness and the project's required quality and\\nsafety properties.\\n\\nStart from the smallest complete solution. Each project supplies its agreed\\nusers, scale, operating conditions and quality constraints. Agreed future needs\\ncan matter; imagined flexibility alone is insufficient. Credible risk analysis\\ncan justify prevention before an incident. Fewer lines, shorter specifications\\nor fewer tests do not establish simplicity; moving work to users, operations\\nor dependencies also has a cost.\\n\\nApply the relevant questions below before definition approval and during design\\nand implementation review. Return unjustified obligations or design complexity\\nfor revision through the existing decision process. Explain material choices\\nin the existing specification, design or review: the need, why a simpler option\\nis insufficient and the added cost. Scale the explanation to the decision; a\\nsmall choice may need one sentence, a significant design may use its existing\\narchitecture decision. Trivial changes need no separate comparison document.\\n\\nThese are human review standards. Lifecycle states and dispositions are preserved.\\n  Approved work orders (WO) remain unchanged; proposed scope changes require\\n  review.\\n- **Transient working material:** The review handoff and evaluator results.\\n  Retain these under the same conditions as [Describe the intended outcome](DEFINE_CHANGE.md#describe-the-intended-outcome).\\n\\n**Actions:**\\n\\n1. Inspect the implementation and follow its callers, references and consumers.\\n   Check tests and fixtures, instructions and documentation, packaging and CI,\\n   and verification outputs where they apply. Keep the working inventory in\\n   the conversation or temporary storage outside the repository.\\n   Group each complete, independently verifiable outcome into one bounded\\n   work order with its necessary supporting changes.\\n2. Select an existing work order (WO) in `draft` for a unit only if its scope\\n   matches. Otherwise, create a new work order (WO) with the commands below.\\n3. Complete each draft using the field table below. In Expected change\\n   surface, name the inspected paths and give a short reason for each.\\n   Explain non-applicable areas and unknown destinations briefly.\\n4. Check that every selected requirement (REQ) has the specification (SPEC)\\n   and verification contract (VER) coverage defined in [Complete work scope](WORK_AND_EVIDENCE.md#complete-work-scope).\\n5. Apply `## work_order` \\u2192 `### Checklist` in\\n   `docs/engineering/ARTIFACT_AUTHORING.md`.\\n6. Add the new work order (WO) IDs to applicable `threatens` relations in risks\\n   (RISK) and `concerns` or `blocks` relations in open decisions (DEC). Keep\\n   every blocked ID in `concerns`. Preserve any required pairing between the\\n   risk (RISK) and decision (DEC).\\n7. If the planned change needs a missing release or operating contract,\\n   use [Prepare a release or operating contract](DRAFT_DEFINITIONS.md#prepare-a-release-or-operating-contract) now that the work\\n   order IDs exist. Include its draft in the proposed package.\\n8. Run the validation command below.\\n9. Correct findings within the proposed drafts. Rerun validation after edits.\\n   Report unrelated findings separately. Retain findings that need a decision\\n   or an accepted-definition amendment as unresolved dependencies.\\n10. Run the selected-work check below for each proposed work order (WO).\\n    Supply every planned file from Expected change surface with\\n    `--planned-path`. Inspect `scope.preparation`: resolve uncovered or\\n    invalid paths in the draft before requesting approval. A successful\\n    command exit does not establish coverage or a complete impact analysis.\\n    Explain future generated destinations using\\n    [Generated outputs](WORK_AND_EVIDENCE.md#generated-outputs).\\n11. Present the outcome, scope, artifact IDs and paths, validation results,\\n    unresolved decisions (DEC), and unapplied amendments. Include the\\n    evaluator's reported next action for each selected work order (WO).\\n\\n| Field or section | Content |\\n| --- | --- |\\n| `Objective` | The outcome this work order (WO) will deliver. |\\n| `In scope` / `Out of scope` | The boundaries from [Define the limits of the change](DEFINE_CHANGE.md#define-the-limits-of-the-change) assigned to this work order (WO). |\\n| `Constraints` | The conditions from [Define the limits of the change](DEFINE_CHANGE.md#define-the-limits-of-the-change) that apply to this work. |\\n| `Expected change surface` | Inspected planned paths, the reason for each, and unresolved destinations. |\\n| `[execution_scope].paths` | Exact repository-relative files or component directories ending in `/`. Use forward slashes; no wildcards, absolute paths, or `..`. |\\n| `Authorized decision envelope` | What the implementer may decide and what requires another accountable decision. |\\n| `Required verification` / `Evidence to record` | The checks and retained outputs defined in [Define how the result will be verified](DRAFT_DEFINITIONS.md#define-how-the-result-will-be-verified). |\\n| `Stop and escalate conditions` | The conditions that require pausing this work. |\\n| `Completion report format` | The required report of completed behavior, checks, limitations, and next accountable decision. |\\n| `[assurance]` | `commit_bound_verification`, its `rationale`, and `decided_by`. Apply the classification rules in [Assurance classification](WORK_AND_EVIDENCE.md#assurance-classification). Record the human who confirmed the classification; an agent may propose it and record that human's decision. |\\n| `[relations]` | `implements` \\u2192 requirements (REQ); `specifications` \\u2192 specifications (SPEC); `verification` \\u2192 verification contracts (VER); `architecture` \\u2192 applicable architectures (ARCH) and required architecture decisions (ADR). |\\n\\nFor PR-based work, include the proposed [review-publication grant](AUTHORITY.md#review-publication-authority)\\nin the decision envelope. Name its repository, source branch, target branch\\nand selected work. Include both the ready-record publication and the later\\ndecision-only push in the expected scope. Present that grant explicitly with\\nimplementation approval; do not infer it from ordinary work authority.\\n\\nIf the assurance classification has not been confirmed, present it as a\\nproposal in the review handoff. Report the missing decision. Do not fill\\n`decided_by` with the draft author's name or an example identity to satisfy\\nvalidation. The human may confirm the classification while reviewing the\\nwork order; record that decision before previewing approval.\\n\\n**Harness commands:**\\n\\nFor each new work order (WO), run the creation sequence from [Draft any missing definitions](DRAFT_DEFINITIONS.md#draft-any-missing-definitions).\\nInspect the preview before creation; replace `WO-ID` with its `allocated_id`:\\n\\n```text\\nharnessctl create-artifact REPO --domain DOMAIN --type work_order --dry-run --json\\nharnessctl create-artifact REPO --domain DOMAIN --type work_order --id WO-ID --json\\n```\\n\\nValidate the formal artifacts after completing the drafts and links:\\n\\n```text\\nharnessctl validate REPO --json\\n```\\n\\nInspect each proposed work order (WO) using its actual ID:\\n\\n```text\\nharnessctl check REPO --artifact WO-ID --json\\n```\\n\\nTo assess the proposed file list, repeat `--planned-path` for every file:\\n\\n```text\\nharnessctl check REPO --artifact WO-ID --planned-path PATH --planned-path ANOTHER-PATH --json\\n```\\n\\nThis option is available only when the selected released evaluator supports\\nit. It reports explicit\\nscope matches, existing automatic admission, uncovered paths and invalid\\ndeclarations. Every unresolved dependency is\\nidentified. The executor may not change source code, modify approved definitions, or authorize a release.\n\n## Constraints\n\nThe source fixture is read-only. All actors are synthetic test labels.\n\n## Expected change surface\n\n| Path | Reason |\n| --- | --- |\n| `src/greeting.py` | Existing source read by the assertion; no modification planned |\n| `docs/engineering/lifecycle-pilot/evidence/WO-P3-001/assertion.json` | Retained assertion result from VER-P3-900 execution |\n\n## Required verification\n\nRun VER-P3-900 on the exact clean fixture commit. The greeting assertion checks that `greeting()` returns exactly `Hello rehearsal`.\n\n## Evidence to record\n\nRetain the actual assertion result in `docs/engineering/lifecycle-pilot/evidence/WO-P3-001/assertion.json`.\n\n## Stop and escalate conditions\n\nStop when the released evaluator refuses or the assertion fails.\n\n## Completion report format\n\nState the completed assertion, its pass or fail result, the retained evidence path, and the next accountable decision.\n"

{
  "tool_use_id": "toolu_01A2uDUT9AwM36pNfZMKopEg",
  "type": "tool_result",
  "content": "{\"resources\": [{\"resource\": \"docs/engineering/harness/AUTHORIZE_WORK.md\", \"path\": \"C:\\\\Users\\\\mathi\\\\Documents\\\\Codex\\\\2026-09-20\\\\verity-plane-plugin-verity-plane-se\\\\work\\\\hag-hosted-next28-20261011\\\\claude-232\\\\native\\\\inputs\\\\released-resources\\\\docs\\\\engineering\\\\harness\\\\AUTHORIZE_WORK.md\", \"release\": {\"archive_sha256\": \"cb35c3c4eb51fe0fca575a9340b1cba6cd63b5f7133a0ebeb3fd32b4ed863053\", \"payload_sha256\": \"0e7b7c2bc10b06150c6c1ebe88d796d047f5d906bdfb5ef4e00914a56c79e7ff\", \"version\": \"0.22.1\"}, \"sha256\": \"991b526d6280554dcca6901c1790e5519b8894e08635a1f10618aa84a29aaf28\", \"bytes\": 15212, \"sections\": [{\"heading\": \"assess-the-proposed-artifact-package\", \"title\": \"Assess the proposed artifact package\", \"anchor\": \"assess-the-proposed-artifact-package\", \"start_line\": 32, \"end_line\": 83, \"content\": \"### Assess the proposed artifact package\\n\\n**Inputs:** The selected work order (WO) IDs, proposed definitions, validation\\nresults, and existing decision records (DEC).\\n\\n**Output:**\\n\\n- **Formal artifacts:** None created or modified.\\n- **Transient working material:** A review list identifying the selected\\n  artifacts, their states, applicable checks, and reported blockers.\\n\\n**Actions:**\\n\\n1. Run validation for the repository.\\n2. Inspect each selected work order (WO) with the command below.\\n3. Read the work order (WO) and its returned reading manifest.\\n4. Review the governing definitions against the relevant checklists in\\n   `docs/engineering/ARTIFACT_AUTHORING.md`.\\n5. Check that the proposed work has defined scope, verification coverage,\\n   assurance classification, and accountable humans for the required decisions.\\n6. List the blockers affecting the selected package. Keep unrelated findings\\n   separate.\\n\\n**Harness commands:**\\n\\n```text\\nharnessctl validate REPO --json\\nharnessctl check REPO --artifact WO-ID --json\\nharnessctl check REPO --artifact WO-ID --checkpoint transition --target approved --json\\n```\\n\\nRead the result of each required check. A `pass` satisfies that check only;\\nit does not approve the artifacts or authorize implementation. If a required\\ncheck returns `fail` or `not_assessable`, report its identifier and stated\\nreason, then follow \\u201cIf a procedure is blocked.\\u201d Do not proceed with the\\naffected approval.\\n\\nThe last command evaluates work-order approval gates. A checkpoint-free\\n`check` only reports lifecycle context. Definition artifacts cannot be selected\\nwith `check`; their transition gates are evaluated by the previews in [Apply the selected transitions](AUTHORIZE_WORK.md#apply-the-selected-transitions).\\n\\nBefore proposing a definition for approval, resolve its template placeholders.\\nIf it contains an `Open decisions` section, confirm that no unresolved decision\\nremains before setting that section to `None`. Do not remove an unresolved\\nquestion merely to make validation pass.\\n\\n**Completion:** The selected package has an explicit list of review findings\\nand approval blockers. No approval is inferred from these checks.\\n\\n**Later use:** [Resolve approval blockers](AUTHORIZE_WORK.md#resolve-approval-blockers) resolves the blockers. [Obtain the human's decision](AUTHORIZE_WORK.md#obtain-the-humans-decision) presents the package for a\\nhuman decision.\\n\\n\", \"content_sha256\": \"2ea6b051236e13dd427317722d16ce7941ef5a4eb4789f7fdf568ab3d0cae3c0\"}, {\"heading\": \"apply-the-selected-transitions\", \"title\": \"Apply the selected transitions\", \"anchor\": \"apply-the-selected-transitions\", \"start_line\": 221, \"end_line\": 270, \"content\": \"### Apply the selected transitions\\n\\n**Inputs:** The exact artifact IDs, target states, accountable human identities,\\nreviewed file digests, decisions from [Obtain the human's decision](AUTHORIZE_WORK.md#obtain-the-humans-decision), and clarification answers from\\n[Define the change](DEFINE_CHANGE.md#procedure) that must be retained in the transition reason.\\n\\n**Output:**\\n\\n- **Formal artifacts:** Only explicitly selected definitions and work orders\\n  (WO) receive the applied state and lifecycle-history entries.\\n- **Transient working material:** The preview and apply results, including\\n  any refused transition.\\n\\n**Actions:**\\n\\n1. Confirm that the reviewed artifact contents still match the human decision.\\n2. Select only the named transitions. Approve governing definitions before\\n   dependent work, or use one explicitly selected packet for mutually\\n   dependent definitions when the evaluator permits it.\\n3. Preview the transition command below.\\n4. Compare every selected ID, target state, actor, and reported effect with\\n   the human decision. Resolve failed gates before proceeding.\\n5. Recheck reviewed definition digests immediately before applying.\\n6. Repeat the passing command with `--apply`. Do not add related artifacts\\n   that the human did not select.\\n\\n**Harness commands:**\\n\\n```text\\nharnessctl transition REPO --set ARTIFACT-ID=TARGET --decision ARTIFACT-ID=ACTOR --json\\nharnessctl transition REPO --set ARTIFACT-ID=TARGET --decision ARTIFACT-ID=ACTOR --apply --json\\n```\\n\\nFor each clarification below the formal-decision threshold, add\\n`--reason \\\"ARTIFACT-ID=Question and confirmed answer\\\"` to both the preview\\nand apply command. Use the actual question and answer. When one artifact\\nhas several such answers, combine them into one reason value for that ID.\\nKeep each complete `ID=TEXT` value as one argument. Confirm the saved reason\\nin the resulting lifecycle entry before discarding the working notes.\\n\\nFor approval, `TARGET` is `approved`. For rejection, use `rejected` and add\\n`--reason \\\"ARTIFACT-ID=Rejection reason\\\"` to both commands. Rejection is\\nterminal. For a packet, repeat `--set` and `--decision` for every explicitly\\nselected artifact. Preview and apply must contain the same assignments.\\n\\n**Completion:** The evaluator reports the actual applied transitions, or the\\nspecific refusal. A successful preview alone changes nothing.\\n\\n**Later use:** [Confirm the authorization result](AUTHORIZE_WORK.md#confirm-the-authorization-result) confirms the resulting authority and readiness.\\n\\n\", \"content_sha256\": \"c5215dec9f35d1a99bf6e4d658a594a31dc544131e70db49b1b8f79514e124b8\"}]}, {\"resource\": \"docs/engineering/harness/EXECUTE_WORK.md\", \"path\": \"C:\\\\Users\\\\mathi\\\\Documents\\\\Codex\\\\2026-09-20\\\\verity-plane-plugin-verity-plane-se\\\\work\\\\hag-hosted-next28-20261011\\\\claude-232\\\\native\\\\inputs\\\\released-resources\\\\docs\\\\engineering\\\\harness\\\\EXECUTE_WORK.md\", \"release\": {\"archive_sha256\": \"cb35c3c4eb51fe0fca575a9340b1cba6cd63b5f7133a0ebeb3fd32b4ed863053\", \"payload_sha256\": \"0e7b7c2bc10b06150c6c1ebe88d796d047f5d906bdfb5ef4e00914a56c79e7ff\", \"version\": \"0.22.1\"}, \"sha256\": \"f094e7bc796ba1002c3fb2848adaccce6c4887e3d92503911bcafa4504a644fe\", \"bytes\": 11979, \"sections\": [{\"heading\": \"start-the-selected-work\", \"title\": \"Start the selected work\", \"anchor\": \"start-the-selected-work\", \"start_line\": 70, \"end_line\": 104, \"content\": \"### Start the selected work\\n\\n**Inputs:** The approved work order (WO), actual executor identity, and\\ncurrent execution context.\\n\\n**Output:**\\n\\n- **Formal artifacts:** The selected work order (WO) becomes `in_progress`\\n  with a lifecycle-history entry when start succeeds.\\n- **Transient working material:** Start-check and transition results.\\n\\n**Actions:**\\n\\n1. If the work order (WO) is already `in_progress`, inspect the recorded start\\n   and resume unapplied work. Do not replay the start transition.\\n2. Otherwise, run start preflight under the existing approval.\\n3. Preview the transition to `in_progress` using the actual executor identity.\\n4. Apply the same transition only when preflight and the preview pass.\\n5. Inspect the resulting state.\\n\\n**Harness commands:**\\n\\n```text\\nharnessctl preflight REPO --work-order WO-ID --phase start --json\\nharnessctl transition REPO --set WO-ID=in_progress --decision WO-ID=ACTOR --json\\nharnessctl transition REPO --set WO-ID=in_progress --decision WO-ID=ACTOR --apply --json\\nharnessctl check REPO --artifact WO-ID --json\\n```\\n\\n**Completion:** The selected work order (WO) is `in_progress`, or its start\\nis blocked with an explicit reason. Existing approval covers a permitted\\nstart; no duplicate human start decision is required.\\n\\n**Later use:** [Implement the approved scope](EXECUTE_WORK.md#implement-the-approved-scope) performs the authorized implementation.\\n\\n\", \"content_sha256\": \"2275126fa8b418eccbaacb1972d0140709337470ba8d190b6566fd16854328d1\"}, {\"heading\": \"retain-the-implementation-evidence\", \"title\": \"Retain the implementation evidence\", \"anchor\": \"retain-the-implementation-evidence\", \"start_line\": 155, \"end_line\": 210, \"content\": \"### Retain the implementation evidence\\n\\n**Inputs:** The implemented changes, verification contracts (VER), required\\nrepository checks, and work-order evidence locations.\\n\\n**Output:**\\n\\n- **Retained evidence files:** Actual check results, review findings, their\\n  resolution, and Git-derived handoff evidence at authorized evidence paths.\\n- **Formal artifacts:** Evidence references may be added where the work order\\n  (WO) requires them. No lifecycle state changes here.\\n- **Transient working material:** A summary of remaining evidence gaps.\\n\\n**Actions:**\\n\\n1. Run every required check using its specified inputs and environment.\\n2. Retain the command arguments, working directory, runtime identity, exit\\n   status, output, and relevant file paths or digests.\\n3. Preserve failed results alongside later corrections and successful reruns.\\n4. Retain the material diff-review findings and their resolution.\\n5. Confirm that all required evidence files exist at the locations named by\\n   the work order (WO) or verification contract (VER).\\n6. Run review preflight.\\n7. Run the Git-derived handoff check with the trusted `BASE` from [Establish the execution context](EXECUTE_WORK.md#establish-the-execution-context).\\n8. Inspect its written evidence and reported findings. Correct in-scope gaps\\n   and rerun affected checks.\\n\\n**Harness commands:**\\n\\n```text\\nharnessctl preflight REPO --work-order WO-ID --phase review --json\\nharnessctl check REPO --artifact WO-ID --checkpoint handoff --from-git BASE --json\\n```\\n\\nFor ordinary files listed in the work order\\u2019s `evidence_paths`, confirm that\\neach file exists inside the repository and contains the required evidence.\\nThese files do not need a machine header. Use the supported evidence command\\nfor generated packets; do not edit their evaluator-owned binding fields.\\n\\nThe Git-derived handoff command writes retained evidence. Confirm its output\\npaths are covered by the approved preparation scope. If the evaluator calls\\nfor creating or rebinding the handoff evidence packet, use:\\n\\n```text\\nharnessctl evidence REPO --artifact WO-ID --checkpoint handoff --json\\n```\\n\\nThis command also writes a file. It does not run tests or make an unsupported\\nclaim true. Rerun the required handoff check after correcting the evidence gap.\\n\\n**Completion:** Required implementation checks pass and the actual evidence\\nis retained, or the exact missing or failed checks remain visible.\\n\\n**Later use:** [Record implementation completion](EXECUTE_WORK.md#record-implementation-completion) requires passing handoff results. Verification later\\nassesses these retained files; they are not disposable planning notes.\\n\\n\", \"content_sha256\": \"4cccfe03ac5fe219da997051bb91053576f18e67e834f0036f93e9856e8f12a2\"}, {\"heading\": \"record-implementation-completion\", \"title\": \"Record implementation completion\", \"anchor\": \"record-implementation-completion\", \"start_line\": 211, \"end_line\": 257, \"content\": \"### Record implementation completion\\n\\n**Inputs:** Completed work, passing handoff checks, retained evidence, and\\nthe unchanged approved execution scope.\\n\\n**Output:**\\n\\n- **Formal artifacts:** The selected work order (WO) becomes `implemented`\\n  with the actual executor recorded in its lifecycle history.\\n- **Transient working material:** The completion report and current next step.\\n\\n**Actions:**\\n\\n1. Confirm that the work and required evidence are complete.\\n2. Preview the completion transition using the executor's own identity.\\n3. Apply the same transition when the preview passes.\\n4. Inspect the resulting work order (WO).\\n5. Report the completed behavior, checks, limitations, and evaluator's next\\n   action. Preserve failures if completion was refused.\\n\\nUse the passing handoff result from the preceding step. A passing completion\\npreview does not replace the handoff check. If the implementation, governing\\nartifacts, or required evidence changed after handoff, rerun the affected\\nchecks before applying completion. If `harnessctl` refuses the retained\\nhandoff evidence, follow its reported corrective step.\\n\\n**Harness commands:**\\n\\n```text\\nharnessctl transition REPO --set WO-ID=implemented --decision WO-ID=ACTOR --json\\nharnessctl transition REPO --set WO-ID=implemented --decision WO-ID=ACTOR --apply --json\\nharnessctl check REPO --artifact WO-ID --json\\n```\\n\\n**Completion:** The work order (WO) is `implemented`, or its completion is\\nblocked. Completion does not verify, release, or deliver the result.\\n\\n**Later use:** Work requiring commit-bound verification proceeds to\\n[Verify the outcome](VERIFY_OUTCOME.md#establish-the-verification-scope).\\nAfter preparation, PR-based work first follows [Publish the review package](PULL_REQUEST.md#publish-the-review-package).\\nThen use the [verification request](VERIFY_OUTCOME.md#obtain-the-humans-verification-decision)\\nwhen the ready record is eligible and its PR is confirmed. Local-only work\\nuses that verification request without a PR. The completion report introduces no\\nadditional human approval. An implemented work order (WO) classified `not_required`\\nneeds no new verification record (VREC); follow its actual next action and\\nany separately authorized delivery instruction.\\n\\n\", \"content_sha256\": \"e9c897ed3281727176649c1a128308da088e0f04282645d2c43a4c7a9a795649\"}]}]}",
  "is_error": false
}

  "type": "tool_result",
  "content": "{\"resources\": [{\"resource\": \"docs/engineering/harness/RISKS_AND_DECISIONS.md\", \"path\": \"C:\\\\Users\\\\mathi\\\\Documents\\\\Codex\\\\2026-09-20\\\\verity-plane-plugin-verity-plane-se\\\\work\\\\hag-hosted-next28-20261011\\\\claude-232\\\\native\\\\inputs\\\\released-resources\\\\docs\\\\engineering\\\\harness\\\\RISKS_AND_DECISIONS.md\", \"release\": {\"archive_sha256\": \"cb35c3c4eb51fe0fca575a9340b1cba6cd63b5f7133a0ebeb3fd32b4ed863053\", \"payload_sha256\": \"0e7b7c2bc10b06150c6c1ebe88d796d047f5d906bdfb5ef4e00914a56c79e7ff\", \"version\": \"0.22.1\"}, \"sha256\": \"8dbc5fb4bb80fc3c2fbf1ec34a2419e8b5856972485226af46ae324fbd9aca5c\", \"bytes\": 11817, \"sections\": [{\"heading\": \"record-risks-risk-affecting-the-change\", \"title\": \"Record risks (RISK) affecting the change\", \"anchor\": \"record-risks-risk-affecting-the-change\", \"start_line\": 13, \"end_line\": 65, \"content\": \"### Record risks (RISK) affecting the change\\n\\n**Inputs:** The outcome, scope, selected definitions, verification coverage,\\nand existing risks (RISK) found in [Identify the existing artifacts that apply to the change](DEFINE_CHANGE.md#identify-the-existing-artifacts-that-apply-to-the-change).\\n\\n**Output:**\\n\\n- **Formal artifacts:** New risks (RISK) in `raised` for threats not already\\n  covered. A paired decision (DEC) in `open` for each risk created with\\n  `--with-decision`. None created where existing records cover the threats.\\n- **Transient working material:** The selection of reused risks (RISK),\\n  review notes, generated IDs and paths, and links awaiting new work order\\n  (WO) IDs. If no risks are identified, retain that result with the reviewed\\n  scope. Retain this material under the same conditions as [Describe the intended outcome](DEFINE_CHANGE.md#describe-the-intended-outcome).\\n\\n**Actions:**\\n\\n1. Identify what could go wrong within the selected change scope.\\n2. Check whether an existing risk (RISK) already records each threat and its\\n   follow-up. Reference that record instead of creating a duplicate.\\n3. For each new risk (RISK), describe the threat and why it matters.\\n4. Identify its follow-up owner.\\n5. Specify the owner's next action.\\n6. Identify the formal artifacts threatened by it.\\n7. Determine whether an unresolved decision must block those artifacts.\\n8. Preview the risk (RISK) with the command below. Inspect the proposed\\n   content, affected IDs, and destination paths.\\n9. Run the same command with the same arguments, removing only `--dry-run`.\\n10. Read every written file reported by the command. Retain its ID and path\\n    for the review handoff.\\n\\n**Harness command:**\\n\\nReplace the quoted text and `OWNER` with the risk description and identified\\nowner. Repeat `--threatens ARTIFACT-ID` for each threatened artifact. Omit that\\noption if no affected formal artifact exists yet:\\n\\n```text\\nharnessctl raise-risk REPO --domain DOMAIN --title \\\"Risk title\\\" --description \\\"What could go wrong and why it matters\\\" --action \\\"Next follow-up action\\\" --owner OWNER --threatens ARTIFACT-ID --dry-run --json\\n```\\n\\nAdd `--with-decision` only if action 7 identifies a decision (DEC) that must\\nblock the named artifacts. Use the same option in both preview and creation.\\nA risk (RISK) alone does not block work.\\n\\n**Completion:** Identified risks (RISK) have formal records with an owner and\\nnext action. If none were identified, retain `None identified` with the\\nreviewed scope as transient working material.\\n\\n**Later use:** Review any paired decision (DEC) in [Record unresolved decisions (DEC)](RISKS_AND_DECISIONS.md#record-unresolved-decisions-dec). [Prepare work orders (WO) linking the definitions, scope, planned work, and verification requirements](DRAFT_WORK_ORDERS.md#prepare-work-orders-wo-linking-the-definitions-scope-planned-work-and-verification-requirements) uses the\\nrisks (RISK) to define work and stop conditions and adds links that depend on\\nnew work order (WO) IDs.\\n\\n\", \"content_sha256\": \"8431175790f8bf07019c19f9cb51d6ee5186b8bc66fccfbb2665ab99fc5028e9\"}, {\"heading\": \"raised-risks\", \"title\": \"Raised risks\", \"anchor\": \"raised-risks\", \"start_line\": 176, \"end_line\": 187, \"content\": \"## Raised risks\\n\\nA risk (RISK) in `raised` may have no paired decision\\n(DEC). Recording that risk alone does not block work. Use\\n`raise-risk --with-decision` when creating a risk that needs a paired blocking\\ndecision.\\n\\nA raised risk MUST NOT have more than one paired decision in `open` or\\n`deferred`. When that decision exists, it names the risk in `concerns`, and\\nits `blocks` set MUST equal the risk's `threatens` set. Disposing the paired\\ndecision applies its recorded effect to the risk in the same operation.\\nThe blocked artifacts change state only through their own transitions.\\n\", \"content_sha256\": \"dcbef84857f6b71fbfa2099de79f6caa4d82e8977b13465eccb762ffc3a9ea1e\"}]}]}",
  "is_error": false

    "content": "# Native diagnostic report\n\nBounded hosted diagnostic under WO-HAG-009/011.\n\n## Selection\n\n- Project: `6792d0a2-75ef-4eea-b763-2fc273da84df`\n- Authority mode: `sandbox-projection`\n- Test copy: true\n- Evaluator: 0.22.1 (`cb35c3c4eb51fe0fca575a9340b1cba6cd63b5f7133a0ebeb3fd32b4ed863053`)\n- Client: 0.22.2 (`60b270f31ddc7393192b2a8e34438f4bab229163e41b1d3ac86de10b758cff56`)\n- Source commit: `29837fc72d3301e7b59b7c628262f3ad2931517b`\n- Qualification candidate: `dfcf1e976123836f00354a228c8e72a833f2e3cf`\n\n## Comparison base\n\nThe retained Git comparison base from the first accepted lifecycle mutation is `99f53f1ee3bd809ac5dbaa5cf4c3687de435bbe1`.\n\n## Outcomes\n\n### Work order WO-P3-001\n\nCreated, authored, approved, started, and completed to `implemented` status. The work order implements REQ-P3-900, uses SPEC-P3-900, and is verified by VER-P3-900.\n\nLifecycle transitions:\n\n1. `draft` to `approved` by test-owner\n2. `approved` to `in_progress` by test-executor\n3. `in_progress` to `implemented` by test-executor\n\nAll evaluator gates passed at each transition.\n\n### Greeting assertion\n\nThe fixture `greeting()` assertion executed and passed. Exit code 0, stdout: `exact greeting passed`. The assertion confirmed `greeting()` returns exactly `Hello rehearsal` as required by REQ-P3-900.\n\nEvidence retained at `docs/engineering/lifecycle-pilot/evidence/WO-P3-001/assertion.json`.\n\n### Handoff evidence\n\nThe handoff was accepted by the released evaluator. The evidence packet was written at `docs/engineering/lifecycle-pilot/evidence/WO-P3-001/WO-P3-001-handoff.md`. The scope check confirmed all changed paths are within execution scope.\n\n### Risk and decision\n\nRISK-P3-001 raised with paired decision DEC-P3-001. The risk threatens WO-P3-001. DEC-P3-001 was decided by test-owner with option `mitigate` and `mitigated_by: [\"WO-P3-001\"]`. RISK-P3-001 transitioned to `mitigating`; DEC-P3-001 transitioned to `decided` (terminal).\n\n## Governing definitions\n\nThe six governing definitions remain unchanged and were not modified:\n\n| ID | Type | Status |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| INT-P3-900 | intent | approved |\n| CAP-P3-900 | capability | approved |\n| REQ-P3-900 | requirement | approved |\n| SPEC-P3-900 | specification | approved |\n| VER-P3-900 | verification | approved |\n| REL-P3-900 | release_contract | approved |\n\nThe imported WO-P3-900 was not acted upon and remains in `draft` status.\n\n## Failures and recovery\n\nTwo recoverable errors occurred:\n\n1. **draft-open-001:** Attempted to open a draft context with WO-P3-901, which did not exist in the baseline. Recovered by opening the context with the existing WO-P3-900, then creating WO-P3-001 within that context.\n2. **start-preflight-001:** Used `mode: \"preview\"` for a preflight read action. The service refused with `HAG_REMOTE_MALFORMED`. Recovered by correcting the mode to `\"inspect\"` and retrying.\n\n## Refusals\n\nNone.\n\n## Unresolved limits\n\nNone. Verification and release preparation are excluded from this diagnostic.\n\n## Evidence paths\n\n| Evidence | Path |\n| --- | --- |\n| Assertion result | `work/assertion-001.json` |\n| Handoff evidence | `docs/engineering/lifecycle-pilot/evidence/WO-P3-001/WO-P3-001-handoff.md` |\n| Observations snapshot | `work/observations-001.json` |\n| Full observations | `work/native-observations.json` |\n\n## Final state\n\n- WO-P3-001: `implemented`\n- RISK-P3-001: `mitigating`\n- DEC-P3-001: `decided` (terminal)\n- Project version: 10\n- Context version: 8\n"
